ReFS Super-Timeline — win11refs2tspecials.raw
==============================================================================
  Sources merged: USN=0  MLog=4243  $SI-MACB=6200  (sorted by time; rows are not joined across sources:
                  a file is listed under its HOME directory's OID in $SI and USN -- the
                  FileRef high half, kept after a move or hard link -- and MLog the target OID)
  NOTE: USN Journal: Inactive — timeline is $SI + MLog only.
  Caveat: USN and $SI carry exact FILETIMEs. MLog has NO wall-clock time — an MLog row's time is
          the latest time its log payload's records carry (a payload can merge up to 15 redo
          blocks, whose own times can differ), or the paired $SI-update's time for a rename or
          delete that embeds none. MLog times that predate the volume are undated + marked.
          An $SI Accessed event exists only while last-access updating was on.
          An MLog row's event is DERIVED from its records (`mlog --info`). A $SI or
          MLog row shows the live tree's path TODAY (PathToday), not the path at the event; a USN row
          the name its record carries (its path at the event is not read).
  751 MLog row(s) are left out: their log payloads carry no embedded time (an undated payload cannot
  be placed on a timeline; `mlog --parse` lists it).
